  1099→- DESIGN: signCheckpoint(bundle, seq, key) verifies the bundle through the FULL boot courtroom first (never sign an unproven snapshot), replays the prefix ONCE, snapshots it, and HMACs the exact canonical triple {hash, manifestHash, seq} — Ed25519 receipted as v3, unknown algs refuse (CHECKPOINT_MALFORMED). The signature anchors the replay seed through TWO content-address hops (sig → signed manifestHash → carried prefix manifest → seed state hash), so the key is the only trust input. carvePartialCustody is keyless-mechanical and re-derives the prefix manifest from the proven bundle, refusing a checkpoint that doesn't describe it (CHECKPOINT_ANCHOR_MISMATCH) — boundary tamper is caught at BOTH layers. Partial boot = v0 courtroom + one new branch: seed present ⇒ gap legal only under a verified signature; replay runs from the anchored seed over receipts [cpSeq+1..tip]; organ carries custody provenance {kind, signedAt:{seq,hash,manifestHash,alg}, verifiedRange, seed{seq,cells,stateHash}, checkpoint}.
